Ak habái kopiôn in video-labôr—telefôn þrowjan wilei, niman du misl.” Sigvaldr þagkjan sum hrains; is ni ga-þahma. “ƕaírban?” “In Aurum disco,” qiþa, “video-skran habái.”

Is gahaífiþ, gaf usþrô. “ƕeila. ƕaírþ sôkjan. ƕan sunus þiudanins ni fraþjan skalks.” Is afgaggida miþ twans drengôs, skadwans miþ is swaltidun in docks.

Helena ubilahts. “Þû qiþiþ—kopiôn? ist istubniþ?” Ik hlaiha sum arma. “Ni; ak meiljan gaskapjan. Þanuh aftra gaqiman—låtan is usgiban Armin, þanuh gibam swikun.” Helena’s andwairþi ni waila gasaíƕs; ak is nodida. “ƕei.”

Weis aftra du AURUM. Taberna swilt, ak manager (Lt)—gerens tabernae—usstaigida; winda sigla, radio jah gramophon niþrô. Ik gasnikjô is, gaf golda. “Leþiþ mik du gremiôn video,” qiþa. Is ni wiljáu; ak þanuh gold gaf milde brusts. Weis in undarleik kameram: þar magnetophon, video-skrans, kaset-spillarei. Ik us-lagida kasseta Helenais, aftrawas hilpan. Kopîon—jah jah—us auda. Meiljan runôn miþ is namô, sōkei jabai swikun agisþ, ak garawiþ decôrum.

Aftra in docks. Sigvaldr hæriþ us skuggam. Is laistida meina—gabaurgs auggô, is laistja. “Hvar kasseta?” ik atgaf is kopiôn, anaþanida afin: “Gifa Armin.” Is bliggja du drengôm; frawardei ufar-staigand in skipa. Minuta fimf, jah atgaggida Armin—harda skadwô, ak lifda; kinnôs usdrausnô; ak andwairþi þiuhan. Helena qam at, grêt—weila grêt—gahait in brusts is. “Broþar! Broþar!” Is qiþiþ sôs: “Swestar…” ƕaírban mihta gnagan skadus in maþljan mans.

Ak ni galaubja niht marz. Sigvaldr saihwida mik, smiða brohs. “Nu, detektôr, ƕaírban habái weis.” Is walh—ōþal mikils. Reiks qimiþ—mikils, brimma in skuggam, ON drengi hindana. Reiks’s auso aflis, gulþa swaswe suns, ak aúh aíns augôni niþƕast. “Þû,” qiþiþ, “þu detektôr, ni waiht. Kasseta—atgaf, jah þu ni hu.” Is laisa kasetan in kaset-spillarei; ni rödd Armins—ak stimmôn misso: ik usgibida tone, bee—bee—skuggam. Reiks andhulida. “Skalks!” Sigvaldr hláih wans. “Skulds misso—Original ist her.” Is sōkjan du Helena. “Gifa mik brast.”

Ik geisida; in handu meina sak—a mîn saks, OHG. Ik gaf aruh haban. Sigvaldr sôkeins miþ arma—ni þagkjan. In þamma brūk, Armin’s handu fraþida pistola us gardô Reiks (Lt arma): jis skjutida ainam hleinjan in marein. Lugna sekundos; dan sis—baúrgs hrauja—skjóta af drengôm. Ik walhana Helena in skuggam, lagjands bi slabans. Skara. Neon liuhaþ af marein, marein handu swilt—ak blod swiltaþ af hlinja.

Ik gaf þragjan—gaggan in skugga in ufarstaina. Sigvaldr in handu arma; ak jis ni saihwida mik innô. Ik nimida is handu, wraka, saks in saljan—ni in hleina—ak in arma; arma dropja. Is skriþiþ swaswe bjarn. “Dagilaífs,” qiþiþ, “þû bist vargr.” “Jah þû,” qiþa, “ist wairþs skadwô.” Is gargida, ak ufar-lutida; ik ni slô, ni drap; ik bindida.

Reiks, in iddja, ga-huftja Armin; ak in þamma stundi, milicija—þai, þai milicija—qimun friþôsni miþ sirêna. ƕaírban? Helena’s telefôn raita þamma aiwô. Ik ni was sôkeis—ak sôkeins atgaggiþ.

Polis—Grekeis—af-traustiþ; Sigvaldr jah drengi handun bindidun; Reiks, miþ ains augôni niht, af qimiþs usdrazna, ak uslauseiþ armans. Helena standiþ niþra, haldands broþar. “Ik þuk launa,” qiþiþ. Ik qiþa: “Ni mis.” Ik saihwida neon in marein, ak sōkei: ni ƕaírban gildja—ni ƕaírban fama. Ik im—detektôr—jah nahts is miþ mik.

Mitra nahts, wisan niþra in garda meina. Ik atvrida kasseta ƕaírban helidam: þata original. Ik leikariþ in spillareis. Rödd Armins qums. “Dagilaífs, jabai þû hausja, wisan: in kasseta ist ni ains waúrds Reiks. Ist singa—ƕaírban waúrds frawaurhta, namôn, navnjôs, telephonis numerôs.” Ist swaswe grammatik in blôþa. Ik gaqimiþ dagis, frabaida milicija. ƕaírban? þatei weis witan: in baúrgs mikilôns, skadwô ni habái ains fraujan. Skuggans haband þans skuggans, ak fotjôs rinnan andugja longô.

Helena jah Armin gaggun af baúrgs; ni in marein; ak in smalla gardja in Westa-wârd—þar raki ni filu, ak vinum swaswe milda. Is sendiþ mik karta: “Fraujan Dagilaífs, þuk launam miþ liuba—ni golda—ak miþ liuhaþ af augôm in naht. ƕan ðû ganga in skugga, ðû bisaiƕiþ meina augô.” Ik hlô.

ƕan mikils liuhaþ nahts? Neon, radio, video, magnetophon—þai ƕaúrban techna af niþida; þai sind haban electra-age waúrda; ik laisai þan: þai synd sôkeins swaswe liuhaþsa bukôns—butin þis skuggô, mannôns sôkjan frijôn ak wairþan gaiz.

Aftir þizai anstai, ik þagkjan þamma þiudangardi meinaizos. Ik ni im þiudans; ik ni im lärja liuhaþs; ak ik im haírdeis in baúrgs, swaswe qiman skuggans ak gaggan. Ik habái sakja manag, ik gabairþa liuba manag. Jabai ains qêns (Helena) gibrukiþ ƕaírban laiseins, ik gistaigas—jah rahnjan. Swa þan sikls.

Sunjô: weis allai niþan in skuggam nawida—ak jabai waiht-þaúrba ist, jabai ains broþar forleita, jabai qêns liuba baugja handu þuk: þan skadus waírþiþ lauha, jah lauha waírþiþ skadus. Jains erus sumai.

Ik aflagjô kasseta in kistô. In gata, nahts aftra atgaggiþ. Neon—neon—siglja: AURUM. Ak ik man: aurum ni þis brusts meina. Aurum—nahts—jah liuhaþ iups. Ik ga-sôkja in gata, liuhaþ mik faura. Ik ni waiht—ak warjan. Qimiþ ains barn, lauhanda radio. Qimiþ qamplô aurbaga, sōkjands manag. Ik frijoþ ina: “ƕar gaggis?” “Du gardja,” qiþiþ, “þar amiþaba, þar swistar, þar video.” Ik hlô swaswe faþar. “Gagg,” qiþa, “ak ni fraida skuggans. Skuggans sind ni draigô; sind—usdrigkjandans watoþa.”

Ik gengja anþarô gata. Ik hausja magnetophon in auhsnôm baúrgs. Ik hausja sôkeins waúrda, swaswe winda. Ik ni baruggaƕ. Ik im Dagilaífs, detektôr, wisan in 1970s nahts, jah ik ga-waúrhta—ni faúr golda—ak faúr rahts, faúr arma, faúr þizos brusts, þizos mans jah qêns, jah barn. Swaswe weis allai skulans wisan, aiþþau ni; ak ik wiljáu. Swaswe.

Aftra miþmêna-lang naht, ik sat in taberna aftra, filu liuba. Raki—smaka—vinum—liuba—hwila. Taberna gereifs qiþiþ: “Dagilaífs, niþis?” “Ni,” qiþa. “Ik bidja: mikilôns γρᾶμματα in hausjô misso.” Is ni fraþjan. Ak jah—Mitra managai sôkeiþ in waúrdom swayla. Darkness has its alphabet. Ak ik laisai sumîs stafans us ‘A Gothic Grammar’—habái in hausja, ni in tuggô. ƕan jabai ik waúrþa niman, ik anda-nausts laiseins: ai, au, ei—liuhadja sum gauja; jah consonants swaswe s, t, k—skadwjan swaswe gatwôs. Sōkei ist íslenska, slavisca, latinôn jah greekên, jah Turkîska sum salt. Miþ þaim, ik gasmeinida nist Waír, ak nar—navnam: noír.

Skadus at-sitja bi mis. Ik saiƕa in gluggô faúr mis. Ik hausja radio. Ik aírida handu miþ saksa jah lagja framis. Ik wisan.
